by Artifact Studio Jewelry · Christmas 2026

Image prompts for approval

30 listings: 10 rings, 10 bracelets, 10 necklaces in ten Christmas families. Each listing gets one reference hero on warm paper, then 10 Etsy sales frames in its own colour world, with that hero cropped to the piece as the only reference.

Holly

pine green, cranberry red · ring, bracelet, necklace

R01Holly Berry Ring Enamel · pine green + cranberry red · holly top 9 x 7 mm · from $730 in 14K
icy silver blue #B5C9D6 Accent: cranberry red · Skin: light warm Holly carries red and green itself; a cool pale blue lets both enamels and the gold read.
  1. 00

    Reference hero (warm paper)

    Studio product photograph of one fine jewelry piece: a slim polished gold ring with a holly cluster on top, 9 by 7 mm: two pointed holly leaves with gently scalloped edges, each split by a polished gold centre vein and filled with flat deep pine green enamel inside thin polished gold rims, and between them three small round berries 2 mm each filled with flat deep cranberry red enamel; round smooth shank. Solid polished 14K yellow gold with flat, glossy kiln-fired vitreous enamel set flush inside recessed cells, every cell framed by a thin polished gold rim; the enamel is perfectly flat and smooth, not domed, not cabochon, no gradient, no painted detail, no stones. Enamel colour: deep pine green (#1F4D3A), deep cranberry red (#9E1B32). Resting on warm off-white textured paper, soft diffused daylight from the upper left, a gentle natural shadow, shallow depth of field, true-to-life small scale, three-quarter front view from slightly above, centred, calm minimal composition. No props, no hands, no text, no logo, no packaging. Photorealistic high-end commercial jewelry photography, square format.
  2. 01

    Hero on a paper wave

    Etsy thumbnail hero: a sheet of icy silver blue paper curls in one soft wave across the top of the frame over a floor of pale icy silver blue tint paper; this ring (a slim polished gold ring with a holly cluster on top, 9 by 7 mm: two pointed holly leaves with gently scalloped edges, each split by a polished gold centre vein and filled with flat deep pine green enamel inside thin polished gold rims, and between them three small round berries 2 mm each filled with flat deep cranberry red enamel; round smooth shank) stands upright on the pale floor, the motif facing the camera in a three-quarter view, seen from slightly above, in sharp focus at the centre of the frame with generous empty space around it, one clean soft shadow under the curl; one small sprig of fresh fir lies far in the corner, out of focus. No people and no hands in the frame. Colour world, identical in every image of this listing: icy silver blue (#B5C9D6) and its paler tints and deeper shades only, matte paper, knit and velvet surfaces; nothing but the piece adds colour. Shot on a Canon 50 mm lens at f/2.8, ISO 400, soft directional studio key light from the upper left with one clean defined shadow, fine 35 mm film grain, true colour, no heavy retouching. The ring must be an exact copy of the ring in the reference image: same outline, same enamel colours and cell layout, same thin polished gold rims, same gold; do not redesign it, do not add charms, motifs or stones; take only the ring from the reference, never its paper background, camera angle or framing. Enamel is perfectly flat, glossy and flush inside thin polished gold rims, never domed, no gradient, no painted detail, no stones. No text, no numbers, no logo, no watermark, no ruler. Square 1:1.
